   103	// Exit codes for `loop run` (frozen by the loop-run test contract):
   104	// 0 done / awaiting-smoke-approval · 1 live-lock refusal · 2 usage error ·
   105	// 3 escalated · 4 parked.
   106	const EXIT_ESCALATED = 3;
   107	const EXIT_PARKED = 4;
   108	
   109	/** @param {any} g */
   110	const gitHeadOf = (g) => g?.headSha ?? 'none';
   111	/** @param {any} g */
   112	const gitContentOf = (g) => g?.contentDigest ?? dedupeKey(g ?? null);
   113	
   114	/**
   115	 * Acquire the supervisor run lock ATOMICALLY (exclusive create — never
   116	 * check-then-write, which loses a race by clobbering the winner). A live
   117	 * owner refuses; a provably-dead one is reclaimed: its recorded in-flight
   118	 * child groups (.handoff/loop/children.ndjson) are KILLED first so no
   119	 * orphan survives the takeover, then the lock is retried once.
   120	 * Shared by `loop run` and `pipeline run`.
   121	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {string} cmdLabel
   122	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true, release: () => void} | {ok: false, code: number}>}
   123	 */
   124	export async function acquireSupervisorLock(root, io, cmdLabel) {
   125	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   126	  const lockPath = `${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`;
   127	  const childrenPath = `${p.dir}/children.ndjson`;
   128	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   129	  const payload = JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, runId: `sup-${io.pid}` });
   130	
   131	  const tryExclusive = () => {
   132	    // Fast-path an already-present lock (fakes without O_EXCL semantics rely
   133	    // on this); the wx flag below is the REAL exclusivity on a POSIX fs — a
   134	    // competitor that lands between the check and the write throws EEXIST
   135	    // instead of being clobbered.
   136	    if (io.fs.existsSync(lockPath)) return false;
   137	    try {
   138	      io.fs.writeFileSync(lockPath, payload, { flag: 'wx' });
   139	      return true;
   140	    } catch (err) {
   141	      if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') return false;
   142	      throw err;
   143	    }
   144	  };
   145	
   146	  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 2; attempt += 1) {
   147	    if (tryExclusive()) {
   148	      return {
   149	        ok: true,
   150	        release: () => {
   151	          try {
   152	            if (io.fs.existsSync(lockPath)) io.fs.unlinkSync(lockPath);
   153	          } catch {
   154	            // Best effort — a stale lock is recoverable via the dead path.
   155	          }
   156	        },
   157	      };
   158	    }
   159	    const existing = safeReadJson(io.fs, lockPath);
   160	    const other = existing.ok ? existing.value : null;
   161	    const alive = other && typeof other.pid === 'number' && typeof io.processAlive === 'function' ? io.processAlive(other.pid) : true;
   162	    const sameProcess = other && other.pid === io.pid && other.startTime === io.startTime;
   163	    if (!sameProcess && (!other || other.host !== io.host || alive)) {
   164	      io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmdLabel}: another supervisor (pid ${other?.pid ?? 'unknown'} on ${other?.host ?? 'unknown'}, run ${other?.runId ?? '?'}) holds the run lock — refusing a second supervisor\n`);
   165	      return { ok: false, code: 1 };
   166	    }
   167	    // Provably dead (or our own stale lock): reap its recorded in-flight
   168	    // child groups BEFORE anything else runs, then reclaim.
   169	    if (io.fs.existsSync(childrenPath)) {
   170	      const lines = String(io.fs.readFileSync(childrenPath, 'utf8')).split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '');
   171	      /** @type {Map<string, any>} */
   172	      const starts = new Map();
   173	      const retired = new Set();
   174	      for (const line of lines) {
   175	        try {
   176	          const rec = JSON.parse(line);
   177	          if (typeof rec?.endedAt === 'string') retired.add(rec.childId);
   178	          else if (typeof rec?.pgid === 'number') starts.set(rec.childId, rec);
   179	        } catch {
   180	          // A torn record is unreapable — skip it.
   181	        }
   182	      }
   183	      // Kill ONLY genuinely in-flight children (start with no retire): a
   184	      // retired child's pgid may have been recycled by an unrelated live
   185	      // process — killing it would be a pid-reuse casualty (H3).
   186	      for (const [childId, rec] of starts) {
   187	        if (retired.has(childId)) continue;
   188	        if (typeof io.processAlive === 'function' && io.processAlive(rec.pgid)) {
   189	          if (typeof io.processKill === 'function') io.processKill(-rec.pgid, 'SIGKILL');
   190	        }
   191	      }
   192	      try {
   193	        io.fs.unlinkSync(childrenPath);
   194	      } catch {
   195	        // Best effort.
   196	      }
   197	    }
   198	    io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmdLabel}: reclaimed the run lock from a provably-dead supervisor (pid ${other?.pid})\n`);
   199	    try {
   200	      io.fs.unlinkSync(lockPath);
   201	    } catch {
   202	      // Already gone.
   203	    }
   204	  }
   205	  io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmdLabel}: could not acquire the run lock after reclaiming — another supervisor keeps winning\n`);
   206	  return { ok: false, code: 1 };
   207	}
   208	
   209	/**
   210	 * The supervisor state machine: drives the spec's phases through headless
   211	 * role children, records every transition in the journal-replayable loop
   212	 * state, enforces the 5-cap, pauses at the smoke gate, and hands limit
   213	 * deaths to the failover transaction. Children run via the injectable seam
   214	 * `io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild`.
   215	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags @param {any} io
   216	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
   217	 */
   218	async function runLoop(flags, io) {
   219	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
   220	  const specPath = `${root}/loop.json`;
   221	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   222	  const lockPath = `${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`;
   223	
   224	  // Preconditions — before any lock or spawn.
   225	  const rawSpec = safeReadJson(io.fs, specPath);
   226	  if (!rawSpec.ok) {
   227	    return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `no loop.json at ${specPath} — scaffold one with: baton loop init "<goal>"`);
   228	  }
   229	  const { config } = loadConfig(root, io);
   230	  if (!config) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `no readable baton.config.json at ${root} — the role matrix is required`);
   231	  const validated = validateLoopSpec(rawSpec.value, config);
   232	  if (validated.ok !== true) {
   233	    return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `loop.json is invalid:\n  - ${validated.errors.join('\n  - ')}`);
   234	  }
   235	  const spec = validated.spec;
   236	
   237	  // Supervisor run lock — atomic exclusive acquisition; provably-dead owners
   238	  // are reclaimed with their recorded child groups reaped first (B8 + G4).
   239	  const lock = await acquireSupervisorLock(root, io, 'loop run');
   240	  if (lock.ok !== true) return lock.code;
   241	
   242	  // Load or initialize the run state — stamped with {flavor, specDigest} so
   243	  // cross-flavor or changed-spec resumes refuse instead of misaligning (H5).
   244	  const specDigest = dedupeKey(spec.phases);
   245	  let { state } = await loadLoopState(root, io);
   246	  if (state === null) {
   247	    state = { ...initLoopState(spec, io), flavor: 'loop', specDigest };
   248	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
   249	  } else {
   250	    if (typeof state.flavor === 'string' && state.flavor !== 'loop') {
   251	      return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `the persisted run state is flavor '${state.flavor}' — a ${state.flavor} run cannot be resumed as a loop (flavor mismatch); archive .handoff/loop or finish the ${state.flavor} run first`);
   252	    }
   253	    if (typeof state.specDigest === 'string' && state.specDigest !== specDigest) {
   254	      return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'loop.json phases changed since this run started (spec digest mismatch) — resuming would misalign the phase position; archive .handoff/loop to start fresh');
   255	    }
   256	  }
   257	  const runId = state.runId;
   258	  // The lock payload's runId now correlates with the run it guards (H8).
   259	  io.fs.writeFileSync(`${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, runId }));
   260	
   261	  /** Apply one event, journal it, persist the snapshot — one transition. */
   262	  const transition = async (/** @type {any} */ ev) => {
   263	    const next = applyLoopEvent(state, ev);
   264	    const seq = await appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io);
   265	    state = { ...next, journalSeq: seq };
   266	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
   267	    return state;
   268	  };
   269	
   270	  const table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, io);
   271	  const runner = io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild;
   272	  const sessionHint = `loop-${runId}`;
   273	  /** @type {string[]} */
   274	  let avoid = [];
   275	  /** @type {Array<{platform: string, model: string}>} */
   276	  let avoidEntries = [];
   277	  let childSeq = 0;
   278	
   279	  try {
   280	    // `loop resume` — a PARK is resumable; an escalation is operator-only.
   281	    if (flags.__resume === true) {
   282	      if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
   283	        io.stderr.write(`baton loop resume: the run is ESCALATED (gate ${state.escalation?.gate}) — escalation is an operator decision; resolve the findings and start a fresh gate instead\n`);
   284	        return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   285	      }
   286	      if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) {
   287	        await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.RESUME });
   288	        io.stdout.write('baton loop resume: the parked run is running again\n');
   289	      }
   290	    }
   291	
   292	    // --approve-smoke: verify the presented token against RECOMPUTED inputs;
   293	    // drift parks (the human approved a state that no longer exists).
   294	    if (typeof flags['approve-smoke'] === 'string') {
   295	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) {
   296	        return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `--approve-smoke only applies while the run awaits smoke approval (status: ${state.status})`);
   297	      }
   298	      const record = safeReadJson(io.fs, `${p.dir}/smoke-approval.json`);
   299	      if (!record.ok) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'no smoke-approval.json found — run the loop to the smoke gate first');
   300	      const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   301	      const currentInputs = {
   302	        stateDigest: dedupeKey(io.fs.readFileSync(p.state, 'utf8')),
   303	        smokeCmd: String(spec.smoke?.cmd ?? ''),
   304	        smokeOutputDigest: String(record.value?.inputs?.smokeOutputDigest ?? ''),
   305	        gitHead: gitHeadOf(git),
   306	        gitContentDigest: gitContentOf(git),
   307	        childAssignment: String(record.value?.inputs?.childAssignment ?? ''),
   308	      };
   309	      const verify = verifySmokeToken(String(flags['approve-smoke']), currentInputs);
   310	      if (verify.ok !== true) {
   311	        const drifted = 'driftedInputs' in verify ? verify.driftedInputs.join(', ') : 'unknown';
   312	        await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: `smoke approval token is stale — drifted inputs: ${drifted}; re-run the smoke gate` });
   313	        io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: smoke approval REFUSED — inputs drifted since the token was issued (${drifted}); the run is parked for a fresh smoke pass\n`);
   314	        return EXIT_PARKED;
   315	      }
   316	      await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE, token: flags['approve-smoke'], verified: true });
   317	      io.stdout.write('baton loop run: smoke approval verified — continuing the run\n');
   318	    }
   319	
   320	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
   321	      io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: the run is escalated (gate ${state.escalation?.gate}) — see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md\n`);
   322	      return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   323	    }
   324	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) {
   325	      io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: the run is parked — ${state.parkReason ?? 'no reason recorded'}\n`);
   326	      return EXIT_PARKED;
   327	    }
   328	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) {
   329	      io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: awaiting smoke approval — resume with: baton loop run --approve-smoke <token from ${p.dir}/smoke-approval.json>\n`);
   330	      return 0;
   331	    }
   332	
   333	    // ---- The phase loop. ---------------------------------------------------
   334	    while (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING && state.phaseIndex < state.phaseCount) {
   335	      const phase = spec.phases[state.phaseIndex];
   336	      const cap = spec.budgets.iterationCap;
   337	      let findings = '';
   338	      let failoverAttempt = 0;
   339	      /** @type {any | null} */
   340	      let forcedAssignment = null;
   341	      /** @type {string | null} */
   342	      let forcedPrompt = null;
   343	      let phaseDone = false;
   344	
   345	      while (!phaseDone) {
   346	        // The cap gates the SPAWN: at the cap the reducer escalates and a
   347	        // 6th child never starts.
   348	        if ((state.iterations?.[phase.id] ?? 0) >= cap) {
   349	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate: phase.id, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
   350	          atomicWriteText(
   351	            io.fs,
   352	            `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`,
   353	            `# Loop escalation\n\nGate '${phase.id}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap on run ${runId}.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n\nEscalation is operator-only: review and resolve the findings with the operator before any new gate attempt.\n`,
   354	          );
   355	          io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: gate '${phase.id}' hit the ${cap}-iteration cap — escalated (see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md)\n`);
   356	          return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   357	        }
   358	        if (childSeq >= spec.budgets.maxChildrenPerPhase * spec.phases.length) {
   359	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: 'total child budget exhausted' });
   360	          return EXIT_PARKED;
   361	        }
   362	
   363	        const resolved =
   364	          forcedAssignment ??
   365	          (() => {
   366	            const r = resolveRoles({ config, to: 'claude-code', avoid, avoidEntries, probes: null }).assignments[phase.role];
   367	            return r && r.mode !== 'unavailable' ? r : null;
   368	          })();
   369	        if (!resolved) {
   370	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: `no eligible platform/model for role '${phase.role}'` });
   371	          io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: no eligible assignment for role '${phase.role}' — parked\n`);
   372	          return EXIT_PARKED;
   373	        }
   374	        const assignment = { ...resolved, role: phase.role };
   375	        const prompt =
   376	          forcedPrompt ??
   377	          [
   378	            `You are the ${phase.role} for loop run ${runId}, phase '${phase.id}'.`,
   379	            `Goal: ${spec.goal}`,
   380	            spec.constraints.length > 0 ? `Constraints:\n${spec.constraints.map((/** @type {string} */ c) => `- ${c}`).join('\n')}` : '',
   381	            findings ? `The previous attempt was BLOCKED with these findings — address every one:\n${findings}` : '',
   382	            "End with exactly:\nVERDICT: APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NOTES | BLOCKED\nFINDINGS: numbered findings, or 'none'",
   383	          ]
   384	            .filter(Boolean)
   385	            .join('\n\n');
   386	        forcedAssignment = null;
   387	        forcedPrompt = null;
   388	
   389	        childSeq += 1;
   390	        const childId = `${String(childSeq).padStart(3, '0')}-${phase.id}`;
   391	        const logPath = `${p.dir}/children/${childId}.log`;
   392	        ensureDir(io.fs, `${p.dir}/children`);
   393	        const childSpec = buildChildArgv(assignment, prompt, { root });
   394	        const result = await runner(childSpec, {
   395	          timeoutMs: spec.budgets.perRoleTimeoutMin * 60_000,
   396	          graceMs: 10_000,
   397	          logPath,
   398	          maxLogBytes: 1_000_000,
   399	          platform: assignment.platform,
   400	          // Persist the child's process group the INSTANT it spawns — a
   401	          // supervisor crash must leave every in-flight child reap-able by
   402	          // the reclaiming run (G4).
   403	          onStart: (/** @type {{pid: number, pgid: number}} */ info) => {
   404	            appendEntry(io.fs, `${p.dir}/children.ndjson`, { childId, pid: info.pid, pgid: info.pgid, startedAt: io.now() });
   405	          },
   406	        });
   407	        // Retire the child the moment it resolves — a reclaim must never kill
   408	        // a completed child's (possibly OS-recycled) process group (H3).
   409	        appendEntry(io.fs, `${p.dir}/children.ndjson`, { childId, endedAt: io.now() });
   410	
   411	        // Classification reads the child's LOG (the frozen transcript), so a
   412	        // limit banner routes to failover even when a verdict parsed.
   413	        const transcript = io.fs.existsSync(logPath) ? io.fs.readFileSync(logPath, 'utf8') : '';
   414	        const cls = classify({ text: transcript, exitCode: result.exitCode ?? 0, platform: assignment.platform, table }).class;
   415	
   416	        if (cls === 'usage-limit' || cls === 'model-unavailable' || cls === 'other-error' || cls === 'throttle' || cls === 'auth') {
   417	          failoverAttempt += 1;
   418	          const decision = await runFailover({
   419	            root,
   420	            io,
   421	            config,
   422	            role: phase.role,
   423	            assignment,
   424	            transcript,
   425	            exitCode: result.exitCode ?? 1,
   426	            sessionHint,
   427	            probes: null,
   428	            avoid,
   429	            avoidEntries,
   430	            attempt: failoverAttempt,
   431	            loopState: { runId, phaseIndex: state.phaseIndex, iterations: state.iterations, status: state.status },
   432	          });
   433	          if (decision.action === 'park') {
   434	            await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PARK, reason: decision.reason });
   435	            io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: parked — ${decision.reason}${'resumeAt' in decision && decision.resumeAt ? ` (resume around ${decision.resumeAt})` : ''}\n`);
   436	            return EXIT_PARKED;
   437	          }
   438	          if (decision.action === 'relaunch') {
   439	            // Platform avoidance is PER-DEATH, not cumulative: runFailover
   440	            // already avoided the just-died platform when it resolved the
   441	            // relaunch. Accumulating here would exhaust every platform on a
   442	            // chained A→B→A failover and park instead of returning to A
   443	            // (acceptance constraint 2). Entry-level avoidance (dead MODELS)
   444	            // does accumulate — a rejected model stays rejected.
   445	            if (Array.isArray(decision.avoidEntries)) avoidEntries = decision.avoidEntries;
   446	            forcedAssignment = { ...decision.assignment, role: phase.role };
   447	            forcedPrompt = typeof decision.prompt === 'string' && decision.prompt.length > 0 ? decision.prompt : null;
   448	            continue; // relaunch the same phase on the new assignment
   449	          }
   450	          continue; // retry: re-spawn the same phase once more
   451	        }
   452	
   453	        const verdict = String(result.verdict ?? 'BLOCKED');
   454	        if (verdict === 'APPROVED' || verdict === 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES') {
   455	          await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE });
   456	          phaseDone = true;
   457	
   458	          // The smoke gate fires right after the smoke-build phase completes.
   459	          if (phase.id === 'smoke-build' && typeof spec.smoke?.cmd === 'string' && spec.smoke.cmd.length > 0) {
   460	            const [cmd, ...cmdArgs] = spec.smoke.cmd.split(/\s+/);
   461	            let smokeOut = '';
   462	            try {
   463	              const r = await io.execFile(cmd, cmdArgs, { cwd: root });
   464	              smokeOut = `${r?.stdout ?? ''}${r?.stderr ?? ''}`;
   465	            } catch (err) {
   466	              smokeOut = `smoke command failed: ${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err)}`;
   467	            }
   468	            await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT });
   469	            const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   470	            const inputs = {
   471	              stateDigest: dedupeKey(io.fs.readFileSync(p.state, 'utf8')),
   472	              smokeCmd: spec.smoke.cmd,
   473	              smokeOutputDigest: dedupeKey(smokeOut),
   474	              gitHead: gitHeadOf(git),
   475	              gitContentDigest: gitContentOf(git),
   476	              childAssignment: JSON.stringify({ platform: assignment.platform, model: assignment.model }),
   477	            };
   478	            const token = smokeApprovalToken(inputs);
   479	            atomicWriteJson(io.fs, `${p.dir}/smoke-approval.json`, { token, inputs, issuedAt: io.now() });
   480	            atomicWriteText(
   481	              io.fs,
   482	              `${p.dir}/SMOKE-REVIEW.md`,
   483	              `# Smoke gate — human approval required\n\nRun: ${runId}\nCommand: \`${spec.smoke.cmd}\`\nExpected: ${spec.smoke.expect ?? '(unspecified)'}\n\n## Output\n\n\`\`\`\n${smokeOut}\n\`\`\`\n\nApprove with:\n\n    baton loop run --approve-smoke ${token}\n\nThe token is bound to the current state/output/git — any drift refuses it.\n`,
   484	            );
   485	            io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: smoke gate reached — review ${p.dir}/SMOKE-REVIEW.md and approve with --approve-smoke <token>\n`);
   486	            return 0;
   487	          }
   488	        } else {
   489	          // BLOCKED (or unparseable, already coerced to BLOCKED upstream).
   490	          findings = String(result.findings ?? '');
   491	          const after = await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate: phase.id, verdict });
   492	          if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
   493	            atomicWriteText(
   494	              io.fs,
   495	              `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`,
   496	              `# Loop escalation\n\nGate '${phase.id}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap on run ${runId}.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n`,
   497	            );
   498	            io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: gate '${phase.id}' escalated at the cap — see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md\n`);
   499	            return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   500	          }
   501	        }
   502	      }
   503	    }
   504	
   505	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.DONE || state.phaseIndex >= state.phaseCount) {
   506	      io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: done — every phase completed (run ${runId})\n`);
   507	      return 0;
   508	    }
   509	    io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: stopped in status ${state.status}\n`);
   510	    return state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED ? EXIT_PARKED : state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED ? EXIT_ESCALATED : 0;
   511	  } finally {
   512	    // The lock is per-invocation: release on every exit path so a paused run
   513	    // (awaiting approval) can be resumed by the next invocation.
   514	    lock.release();
   515	  }
   516	}

     2	 * Child supervision — the loop's reliability surface. Owns the FULL child
     3	 * lifecycle: argv assembly (per-role permissions), detached process-group
     4	 * spawn with closed stdin, timeout SIGTERM→grace→SIGKILL of the whole group,

    15	// Every review seat is read-only — writers are the implementer-side roles
    16	// only. The merger CHILD is read-only too: it adversarially re-checks, but
    17	// the SUPERVISOR performs the actual merge via worktrees.mergeSubtask

    21	// AGENTS.md §9: children commit solely as the repo owner, zero AI attribution.

    29	/**
    30	 * Assemble the spawn contract for a role child. Reviewers are read-only on
    31	 * every platform; every child is marked supervised (its own baton hooks
    32	 * no-op), carries the sole-author git identity, and has stdin CLOSED at
    33	 * spawn (the codex-exec stdin-hang field failure).
    34	 * @param {{platform: string, role: string, model: string, effort?: string | null, mode?: string}} assignment
    35	 * @param {string} prompt
    36	 * @param {{root: string}} opts
    37	 * @returns {{command: string, args: string[], env: Record<string, string>, stdio: any[], cwd: string}}
    38	 */
    39	export function buildChildArgv(assignment, prompt, opts) {
    40	  const readOnly = REVIEWER_ROLES.has(assignment.role);
    41	  const env = { BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD: '1', ...GIT_IDENTITY };
    42	  const stdio = ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'];
    43	
    44	  if (assignment.platform === 'claude-code') {
    45	    // --model honors the role matrix (and entry-level avoidance);
    46	    // --output-format json is what parseVerdict's claude branch reads; the
    47	    // cwd puts the child in its seat, never the supervisor cwd.
    48	    const args = ['-p', prompt, '--model', assignment.model, '--output-format', 'json'];
    49	    if (readOnly) {
    50	      // Scoped read-only git so a reviewer can actually SEE the diff it
    51	      // reviews — still no write/edit capability.
    52	      args.push('--permission-mode', 'default', '--allowedTools', 'Read,Grep,Glob,Bash(git diff:*),Bash(git log:*),Bash(git show:*)');
    53	    } else {
    54	      args.push('--permission-mode', 'acceptEdits', '--allowedTools', 'Read,Grep,Glob,Write,Edit,Bash');
    55	    }
    56	    return { command: 'claude', args, env, stdio, cwd: opts.root };
    57	  }
    58	
    59	  // codex (and the default shape for exec-style CLIs).
    60	  const args = ['exec', '-C', opts.root, '-s', readOnly ? 'read-only' : 'workspace-write', '--model', assignment.model];
    61	  if (assignment.effort) args.push('-c', `model_reasoning_effort=${assignment.effort}`);
    62	  args.push(prompt);
    63	  return { command: 'codex', args, env, stdio, cwd: opts.root };
    64	}
    65	
    66	const VERDICT_RE = /VERDICT:\s*(APPROVED_WITH_NOTES|APPROVED|BLOCKED)\b/g;
    67	
    68	/** @param {string} region @returns {{verdict: string, findings: string} | null} */
    69	function lastVerdictIn(region) {
    70	  let last = null;
    71	  for (const m of region.matchAll(VERDICT_RE)) last = m;
    72	  if (!last) return null;
    73	  const after = region.slice((last.index ?? 0) + last[0].length);
    74	  return { verdict: last[1], findings: after.trim() };
    75	}
    76	
    77	/**
    78	 * Region-bounded verdict parsing — the prompt region (which quotes the
    79	 * verdict format in its instructions) is NEVER scanned, and there is no
    80	 * whole-transcript fallback. Unparseable is BLOCKED, never APPROVED.
    81	 * - codex: only the text after the LAST line starting with 'tokens used'.
    82	 * - claude-code: only the `result` field of the structured JSON output.
    83	 * Multiple tails inside the trusted region → the LAST one wins.
    84	 * @param {string} transcript @param {{platform: string}} opts
    85	 * @returns {{verdict: string, findings: string, reason?: string}}
    86	 */
    87	export function parseVerdict(transcript, { platform }) {
    88	  const unparseable = { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: '', reason: 'unparseable' };
    89	  const text = String(transcript ?? '');
    90	
    91	  if (platform === 'claude-code') {
    92	    let obj = null;
    93	    try {
    94	      obj = JSON.parse(text.trim());
    95	    } catch {
    96	      return unparseable;
    97	    }
    98	    if (typeof obj?.result !== 'string') return unparseable;
    99	    const hit = lastVerdictIn(obj.result);
   100	    return hit ?? unparseable;
   101	  }
   102	
   103	  // codex: locate the LAST 'tokens used' marker line; the trusted region is
   104	  // strictly after it.
   105	  const lines = text.split('\n');
   106	  let markerLine = -1;
   107	  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
   108	    if (/^tokens used\b/i.test(lines[i])) markerLine = i;
   109	  }
   110	  if (markerLine === -1) return unparseable;
   111	  const region = lines.slice(markerLine + 1).join('\n');
   112	  const hit = lastVerdictIn(region);
   113	  return hit ?? unparseable;
   114	}
   115	
   116	const TRUNCATION_MARKER = '\n[... truncated by baton log cap ...]\n';
   117	
   118	/**
   119	 * Cap a transcript: head preserved, middle replaced with a marker, TAIL
   120	 * preserved verbatim (the verdict lives at the end and must never be cut).
   121	 * @param {string} text @param {number} maxBytes
   122	 * @returns {string}
   123	 */
   124	export function capLog(text, maxBytes) {
   125	  const s = String(text ?? '');
   126	  if (Buffer.byteLength(s) <= maxBytes) return s;
   127	  const headBytes = Math.max(1, Math.floor(maxBytes * 0.4));
   128	  const tailBytes = Math.max(1, maxBytes - headBytes);
   129	  const buf = Buffer.from(s);
   130	  const head = buf.subarray(0, headBytes).toString();
   131	  const tail = buf.subarray(buf.length - tailBytes).toString();
   132	  return head + TRUNCATION_MARKER + tail;
   133	}
   134	
   135	/**
   136	 * Exit classification delegates to core detect — one classifier, one
   137	 * signature table, no drift. Returns exactly classify(...).class.
   138	 * @param {string} transcript @param {number} exitCode @param {string} platform @param {any} table
   139	 * @returns {string}
   140	 */
   141	export function classifyChildExit(transcript, exitCode, platform, table) {
   142	  return classify({ text: transcript, exitCode, platform, table }).class;
   143	}
   144	
   145	/** @param {number} pid @param {NodeJS.Signals} sig */
   146	function killGroup(pid, sig) {
   147	  try {
   148	    process.kill(-pid, sig);
   149	  } catch {
   150	    // ESRCH — already gone.
   151	  }
   152	}
   153	
   154	/**
   155	 * Spawn and supervise one child: detached in its OWN process group, stdin
   156	 * closed; on timeout SIGTERM the group, wait graceMs, SIGKILL the group (no
   157	 * zombie grandchildren). Output streams to a capped log at opts.logPath; the
   158	 * verdict is parsed from the full transcript, region-bounded.
   159	 * @param {{command: string, args: string[], env?: Record<string, string>, cwd?: string}} spec
   160	 * @param {{timeoutMs: number, graceMs: number, logPath: string, maxLogBytes?: number, platform?: string, onStart?: (info: {pid: number, pgid: number}) => void}} opts
   161	 * @returns {Promise<{timedOut: boolean, exitCode: number | null, verdict?: string, findings?: string, reason?: string, logPath: string, pgid?: number}>}
   162	 */
   163	export function superviseChild(spec, opts) {
   164	  return new Promise((resolve) => {
   165	    const maxLogBytes = opts.maxLogBytes ?? 1_000_000;
   166	    const child = spawn(spec.command, spec.args, {
   167	      cwd: spec.cwd,
   168	      env: { ...process.env, ...(spec.env ?? {}) },
   169	      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
   170	      detached: true, // its own process group — the kill unit
   171	    });
   172	
   173	    // Report the group at spawn time so the supervisor can persist it BEFORE
   174	    // the child completes — a supervisor crash leaves the orphan reap-able.
   175	    const pgid = typeof child.pid === 'number' ? child.pid : -1;
   176	    if (typeof opts.onStart === 'function' && typeof child.pid === 'number') {
   177	      opts.onStart({ pid: child.pid, pgid });
   178	    }
   179	
   180	    let out = '';
   181	    let timedOut = false;
   182	    /** @type {NodeJS.Timeout | null} */
   183	    let killTimer = null;
   184	
   185	    const onChunk = (/** @type {Buffer} */ c) => {
   186	      out += c.toString();
   187	    };
   188	    child.stdout?.on('data', onChunk);
   189	    child.stderr?.on('data', onChunk);
   190	
   191	    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
   192	      timedOut = true;
   193	      if (typeof child.pid === 'number') {
   194	        killGroup(child.pid, 'SIGTERM');
   195	        killTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   196	          if (typeof child.pid === 'number') killGroup(child.pid, 'SIGKILL');
   197	        }, opts.graceMs);
   198	      }
   199	    }, opts.timeoutMs);
   200	
   201	    const finish = (/** @type {number | null} */ exitCode) => {
   202	      clearTimeout(timeout);
   203	      if (killTimer) clearTimeout(killTimer);
   204	      // Belt-and-braces: reap any group survivors even on a clean exit path.
   205	      if (timedOut && typeof child.pid === 'number') killGroup(child.pid, 'SIGKILL');
   206	      try {
   207	        mkdirSync(dirname(opts.logPath), { recursive: true });
   208	        writeFileSync(opts.logPath, capLog(out, maxLogBytes));
   209	      } catch {
   210	        // The log must never mask the result.
   211	      }
   212	      const parsed = parseVerdict(out, { platform: opts.platform ?? 'codex' });
   213	      resolve({
   214	        timedOut,
   215	        exitCode: timedOut ? null : exitCode,
   216	        verdict: parsed.verdict,
   217	        findings: parsed.findings,
   218	        ...(parsed.reason ? { reason: parsed.reason } : {}),
   219	        logPath: opts.logPath,
   220	        pgid,
   221	      });
   222	    };
   223	
   224	    child.on('error', () => finish(null));
   225	    child.on('close', (code) => finish(code));
   226	  });
   227	}

     1	/**
     2	 * Pure, explainable role resolution over the committed matrix. Every decision
     3	 * is auditable: chosen entries carry their chainIndex; passed-over entries land
     4	 * in skipped[] with a why. avoid[] population is the caller's job.
     5	 * @param {{config: any, to: string, avoid?: string[], avoidEntries?: Array<{platform: string, model: string}>, nativeOnly?: boolean, probes?: Record<string, {capability: string, outcome: string}> | null}} input
     6	 * @returns {{assignments: Record<string, any>, notes: string[]}}
     7	 */
     8	export function resolveRoles({ config, to, avoid = [], avoidEntries = [], nativeOnly = false, probes = null }) {
     9	  /** @type {string[]} */
    10	  const notes = [];
    11	
    12	  /**
    13	   * The eligibility verdict for a platform, or null when eligible.
    14	   * @param {string} platform @returns {string | null}
    15	   */
    16	  function skipReason(platform) {
    17	    if (avoid.includes(platform)) return 'avoided';
    18	    if (!(platform in (config.platforms ?? {}))) return 'unknown-platform';
    19	    if (config.platforms[platform]?.enabled === false) return 'disabled';
    20	    if (probes && probes[platform]) {
    21	      const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    22	      if (outcome === 'rate-limited') return 'rate-limited';
    23	      if (capability === 'not-installed') return 'not-installed';
    24	      // 'installed' means installation was PROVEN but the probe was capped there
    25	      // (capOnSuccess) — authentication is UNVERIFIED, not verified-failed. Only
    26	      // a probe that ran and did NOT succeed (outcome !== 'ok') is a verified
    27	      // auth/reachability failure worth skipping; a capped success stays
    28	      // selectable and is flagged degraded below (iter-3 F1, plan §Role matrix).
    29	      if (capability === 'installed' && outcome !== 'ok') return 'unauthenticated';
    30	    }
    31	    return null;
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  // A selection is degraded when its availability is UNVERIFIED (gate-2 iter-2
    35	  // M3, iter-3 F1): with no probe cache at all, resolution is offline and EVERY
    36	  // selection is degraded (plan: "Offline resolution always succeeds, flagged
    37	  // degraded"); with a partial cache, platforms the cache is missing are; and a
    38	  // platform probed only to 'installed' (auth unverified, e.g. a --version
    39	  // success) is degraded even though it stays selectable.
    40	  /** @param {string} platform */
    41	  const probeUnverified = (platform) => {
    42	    if (probes == null || !probes[platform]) return true;
    43	    const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    44	    // Degraded when ANY required dimension is unverified: a capped 'installed'
    45	    // (auth never confirmed) OR a non-ok outcome (iter-4 I6) — a selectable
    46	    // platform whose last probe ERRORED or TIMED OUT is presented as
    47	    // reachability-verified otherwise, which is exactly the silent-degradation
    48	    // the plan's degraded flag exists to surface. (rate-limited is skipped, not
    49	    // selected, so it never reaches here.)
    50	    return capability === 'installed' || outcome !== 'ok';
    51	  };
    52	
    53	  // Entry-level avoidance (plan §Model-level failover): a specific
    54	  // {platform, model} tuple is dead (e.g. account-tier rejected) while its
    55	  // platform stays usable. Matching is on model IDENTITY — an @effort suffix
    56	  // on either side is ignored — and requires BOTH dimensions, so the same
    57	  // model name on another platform is never caught.
    58	  /** @param {any} m */
    59	  const baseModel = (m) => (typeof m === 'string' ? m.split('@')[0] : m);
    60	  /** @param {string} platform @param {string} model */
    61	  const entryAvoided = (platform, model) =>
    62	    avoidEntries.some((a) => a?.platform === platform && baseModel(a?.model) === baseModel(model));
    63	
    64	  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
    65	  const assignments = {};
    66	  for (const [role, chain] of Object.entries(config.roles)) {
    67	    /** @type {any[]} */
    68	    const skipped = [];
    69	    /** @type {any} */
    70	    let assignment = null;
    71	
    72	    for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
    73	      const entry = chain[i];
    74	      if (nativeOnly && entry.platform !== to) {
    75	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'non-native' });
    76	        continue;
    77	      }
    78	      const why = skipReason(entry.platform);
    79	      if (why) {
    80	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why });
    81	        continue;
    82	      }
    83	      // After skipReason so a dual match keeps the platform-level label.
    84	      if (entryAvoided(entry.platform, entry.model)) {
    85	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'avoided-entry' });
    86	        continue;
    87	      }
    88	      assignment = {
    89	        platform: entry.platform,
    90	        model: entry.model,
    91	        effort: entry.effort ?? null,
    92	        mode: entry.platform === to ? 'native' : 'delegated',
    93	        chainIndex: i,
    94	        skipped,
    95	      };
    96	      if (probeUnverified(entry.platform)) assignment.degraded = true;
    97	      break;
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    if (!assignment && nativeOnly) {
   101	      // Forced-default is only valid when the destination itself is eligible.
   102	      const whyTo = skipReason(to);
   103	      const model = config.defaults?.[to];
   104	      if (!whyTo && typeof model === 'string') {
   105	        assignment = { platform: to, model, effort: null, mode: 'forced-default', chainIndex: null, skipped };
   106	        if (probeUnverified(to)) assignment.degraded = true;
   107	      }
   108	    }
   109	
   110	    if (!assignment) {
   111	      assignment = { platform: null, model: null, effort: null, mode: 'unavailable', chainIndex: null, skipped };
   112	    }
   113	    assignments[role] = assignment;
   114	  }
   115	
   116	  return { assignments, notes };
   117	}

     1	/**
     2	 * Loop failover orchestration — what the supervisor does when a child dies.
     3	 * Classifies the death via the signature table, then takes exactly one path:
     4	 * retry-once-then-park (generic failures), entry-level re-resolve
     5	 * (model-unavailable — the platform survives its dead model), or the
     6	 * usage-limit transaction: checkpoint the loop position INTO the bundle,
     7	 * seal, and receive onto the resolver's pick with one bounded stale retry.
     8	 * Writes NOTHING under .handoff/loop/ — the supervisor applies the returned
     9	 * decision to loop state afterward. Plan:
    10	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"Limit failover" +
    11	 * §"Model-level failover".
    12	 */
    13	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
    14	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
    15	import { loadSignatures } from '../detect/signatures.mjs';
    16	import { classify } from '../detect/classifier.mjs';
    17	import { resolveRoles } from '../roles/resolve.mjs';
    18	import { loadBundle } from '../bundle/store.mjs';
    19	import { snapshot as gitSnapshot } from '../git/snapshot.mjs';
    20	import { prepare, commit } from '../receive/txn.mjs';
    21	import { cmdCheckpoint } from '../commands/checkpoint.mjs';
    22	import { cmdFinalize } from '../commands/finalize.mjs';
    23	
    24	const BUILTIN_SIGNATURES = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', 'data', 'signatures.v1.json');
    25	
    26	/**
    27	 * @param {{
    28	 *   root: string, io: any, config: any, role: string,
    29	 *   assignment: {platform: string, role?: string, model: string, effort?: string | null, mode?: string},
    30	 *   transcript: string, exitCode: number, sessionHint: string,
    31	 *   probes?: any, avoid?: string[], avoidEntries?: Array<{platform: string, model: string}>,
    32	 *   attempt: number, loopState?: any, degradedOpen?: boolean,
    33	 * }} input
    34	 * @returns {Promise<
    35	 *   | {action: 'retry', class: string, attempt: number}
    36	 *   | {action: 'park', class: string, reason: string, resumeAt?: string}
    37	 *   | {action: 'relaunch', class: string, assignment: any, avoidEntries?: any[], prompt?: string}
    38	 * >}
    39	 */
    40	export async function runFailover(input) {
    41	  const { root, io, config, role, assignment, sessionHint } = input;
    42	  const avoid = input.avoid ?? [];
    43	  const avoidEntries = input.avoidEntries ?? [];
    44	
    45	  // Classification is TABLE-driven — the same loader/classifier as `baton
    46	  // detect`, so overlay/fixture signatures are honored, never hardcoded.
    47	  const table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, io);
    48	  const verdict = classify({ text: input.transcript, exitCode: input.exitCode, platform: assignment.platform, table });
    49	
    50	  // Model-level failure: the platform is fine, one model is dead. Avoid the
    51	  // exact {platform, model} tuple and re-resolve — no seal, no receive.
    52	  if (verdict.class === 'model-unavailable') {
    53	    const nextAvoidEntries = [...avoidEntries, { platform: assignment.platform, model: assignment.model }];
    54	    const resolved = resolveRoles({ config, to: assignment.platform, avoid, avoidEntries: nextAvoidEntries, probes: input.probes ?? null });
    55	    const next = resolved.assignments[role];
    56	    if (!next || next.mode === 'unavailable') {
    57	      return { action: 'park', class: verdict.class, reason: `no eligible chain entry left for role '${role}' after avoiding ${assignment.platform}/${assignment.model}` };
    58	    }
    59	    return { action: 'relaunch', class: verdict.class, assignment: next, avoidEntries: nextAvoidEntries };
    60	  }
    61	
    62	  // Anything that is not a usage-limit death is a child failure, not a
    63	  // platform death: retry once, then park. Never a seal or receive.
    64	  if (verdict.class !== 'usage-limit') {
    65	    if (input.attempt <= 1) return { action: 'retry', class: verdict.class, attempt: input.attempt };
    66	    return { action: 'park', class: verdict.class, reason: `child failed twice (${verdict.class}) — parked for operator review` };
    67	  }
    68	
    69	  // ---- Usage-limit: the exact transaction (plan steps 3–8). ----------------
    70	  const { bundle } = loadBundle(root, io);
    71	  const originPlatform = bundle?.origin?.platform ?? assignment.platform;
    72	
    73	  // (3) Checkpoint the loop position INTO the bundle under the supervisor
    74	  // session BEFORE any seal, so the frozen seal carries it.
    75	  if (input.loopState !== undefined) {
    76	    const checkpointIo = {
    77	      ...io,
    78	      stdin: JSON.stringify({
    79	        schema: 'baton/event@1',
    80	        events: [{ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: `loop position at limit death: ${JSON.stringify(input.loopState)}` } }],
    81	      }),
    82	    };
    83	    await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', originPlatform, '--session', sessionHint, '--root', root], checkpointIo);
    84	  }
    85	
    86	  // (4) Seal — unless the caller knows a clean seal is impossible
    87	  // (degradedOpen: a prior death left the bundle unsealed; receive's
    88	  // degraded-open path compensates), or the bundle is already sealed.
    89	  const alreadySealed = bundle?.handoff?.status === 'sealed';
    90	  if (input.degradedOpen !== true && !alreadySealed) {
    91	    await cmdFinalize(['--reason', 'usage-limit death during a supervised loop run', '--reason-class', 'usage-limit', '--root', root], io);
    92	  }
    93	
    94	  // (7, ordered before receive because receive needs the target) — resolver
    95	  // pick with the dead origin platform avoided.
    96	  const relaunchAvoid = avoid.includes(assignment.platform) ? avoid : [...avoid, assignment.platform];
    97	  const resolved = resolveRoles({ config, to: assignment.platform, avoid: relaunchAvoid, avoidEntries, probes: input.probes ?? null });
    98	  const target = resolved.assignments[role];
    99	  if (!target || target.mode === 'unavailable') {
   100	    return {
   101	      action: 'park',
   102	      class: verdict.class,
   103	      reason: `every platform is avoided or unavailable for role '${role}' — parked with a resume hint`,
   104	      ...(verdict.resetHint ? { resumeAt: verdict.resetHint } : {}),
   105	    };
   106	  }
   107	
   108	  // (5–6) Receive prepare+commit back-to-back with IDENTICAL intake and the
   109	  // supervisor session hint. Git is re-derived fresh for each phase (exactly
   110	  // as the CLI does), so real drift inside the window stales the token; ONE
   111	  // automatic re-prepare retry, then park. No .handoff/loop writes anywhere.
   112	  const receiveOpts = (/** @type {any} */ git) => ({
   113	    platform: target.platform,
   114	    origin: 'unknown', // defaults from the sealed bundle's recorded origin
   115	    reason: 'unspecified', // defaults from the sealed reason
   116	    reasonClass: 'usage-limit',
   117	    gitSnapshot: git,
   118	    probes: input.probes ?? null,
   119	    sessionHint,
   120	    sessionUnstable: false,
   121	  });
   122	
   123	  /** @type {string} */
   124	  let prompt = '';
   125	  let lastError = null;
   126	  for (let window = 1; window <= 2; window += 1) {
   127	    const prepGit = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   128	    const prepared = prepare(root, receiveOpts(prepGit), io);
   129	    prompt = prepared.prompt;
   130	    const commitGit = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   131	    try {
   132	      commit(root, prepared.token, receiveOpts(commitGit), io);
   133	      lastError = null;
   134	      break;
   135	    } catch (err) {
   136	      lastError = err;
   137	    }
   138	  }
   139	  if (lastError !== null) {
   140	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (lastError)?.message ?? String(lastError);
   141	    return { action: 'park', class: verdict.class, reason: `receive commit stayed stale after one re-prepare retry — ${msg}` };
   142	  }
   143	
   144	  // (8) Relaunch on the adopted platform with the resume prompt.
   145	  return {
   146	    action: 'relaunch',
   147	    class: verdict.class,
   148	    assignment: target,
   149	    avoidEntries,
   150	    prompt,
   151	  };
   152	}

     1	/**
     2	 * Guarded worktree transaction layer (Layer 3) — every git operation is a
     3	 * checked transaction: namespaced branches only, pre/postflight assertions
     4	 * around each child, merges under a supervisor merge lock gated on a
     5	 * full-range attribution scan, ff-only seat syncs, prune self-heal, and a
     6	 * branch jail so nothing outside baton/wt-* is ever touched. Plan:
     7	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"Worktree transaction
     8	 * layer" + §"Attribution enforcement for child commits".
     9	 */
    10	import { ensureIgnoreLine } from '../scaffold/gitignore.mjs';
    11	import { atomicWriteJson, ensureDir } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
    12	
    13	export const WORKTREE_BRANCH_RE = /^baton\/wt-(a|b)\//;
    14	
    15	const SEATS = /** @type {const} */ (['a', 'b']);
    16	const SOLE_AUTHOR = { name: 'SakshamUboweja', email: 'ssakshamu@gmail.com' };
    17	
    18	// Mirrors the doctor git-guard trailer discipline (core/src/commands/doctor.mjs).
    19	const TRAILER_PATTERNS = [
    20	  /co-authored-by:[^\n]*\b(claude|gpt|copilot|codex)\b/i,
    21	  /generated (with|by)[^\n]*\bclaude\b/i,
    22	  /\u{1F916}/u,
    23	];
    24	
    25	// One declared log format — per-commit fields NUL-delimited in this exact
    26	// order (sha, author name/email, committer name/email, body), records
    27	// RS-terminated. The parser below consumes exactly this shape.
    28	const LOG_FORMAT = '%H%x00%an%x00%ae%x00%cn%x00%ce%x00%B%x1e';
    29	
    30	/** @param {string} root */
    31	export function worktreePaths(root) {
    32	  const dir = `${root}/.worktrees`;
    33	  /** @type {Record<string, string>} */
    34	  const seats = { a: `${dir}/wt-a`, b: `${dir}/wt-b` };
    35	  return { dir, seats };
    36	}
    37	
    38	/** @param {string} seat */
    39	const seatBranch = (seat) => `baton/wt-${seat}/base`;
    40	
    41	/** @param {any} io @param {string} cwd @param {string[]} args */
    42	async function git(io, cwd, args) {
    43	  return io.execFile('git', args, { cwd });
    44	}
    45	
    46	/** Parse `git worktree list --porcelain` into [{path, branch}]. @param {string} out */
    47	function parseWorktreeList(out) {
    48	  /** @type {Array<{path: string, branch: string | null}>} */
    49	  const entries = [];
    50	  for (const block of String(out).split(/\n\n+/)) {
    51	    const path = block.match(/^worktree (.+)$/m)?.[1];
    52	    if (!path) continue;
    53	    const branch = block.match(/^branch refs\/heads\/(.+)$/m)?.[1] ?? null;
    54	    entries.push({ path, branch });
    55	  }
    56	  return entries;
    57	}
    58	
    59	/**
    60	 * Ensure both seat worktrees exist on their namespaced branches and that
    61	 * `.worktrees/` is gitignored. Idempotent: an existing seat is left alone.
    62	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
    63	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, worktrees: Array<{seat: string, path: string, branch: string}>, gitignoreChanged: boolean}>}
    64	 */
    65	export async function setupWorktrees(root, io) {
    66	  const p = worktreePaths(root);
    67	
    68	  const gitignorePath = `${root}/.gitignore`;
    69	  const existing = io.fs.existsSync(gitignorePath) ? io.fs.readFileSync(gitignorePath, 'utf8') : '';
    70	  const ensured = ensureIgnoreLine(existing, '.worktrees/');
    71	  if (ensured.changed) io.fs.writeFileSync(gitignorePath, ensured.text);
    72	
    73	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
    74	  /** @type {Array<{seat: string, path: string, branch: string}>} */
    75	  const worktrees = [];
    76	  for (const seat of SEATS) {
    77	    const path = p.seats[seat];
    78	    const present = listed.find((e) => e.path === path);
    79	    if (present) {
    80	      worktrees.push({ seat, path, branch: present.branch ?? seatBranch(seat) });
    81	      continue;
    82	    }
    83	    const branch = seatBranch(seat);
    84	    await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'add', '-b', branch, path]);
    85	    worktrees.push({ seat, path, branch });
    86	  }
    87	  return { ok: true, worktrees, gitignoreChanged: ensured.changed };
    88	}
    89	
    90	/**
    91	 * Preflight before a child runs in a seat: the worktree is listed, its index
    92	 * is clean, and HEAD sits on the expected branch. Refusals name the failed
    93	 * check and never issue destructive git.
    94	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string}} target @param {any} io
    95	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, check: string, refusal: string}>}
    96	 */
    97	export async function preflightWorktree(root, target, io) {
    98	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
    99	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
   100	  if (!listed.some((e) => e.path === path)) {
   101	    return { ok: false, check: 'missing-worktree', refusal: `worktree ${path} is not listed by git — run setup or self-heal first` };
   102	  }
   103	  const status = (await git(io, path, ['status', '--porcelain'])).stdout.trim();
   104	  if (status.length > 0) {
   105	    return { ok: false, check: 'dirty-index', refusal: `worktree ${path} has a dirty index/working tree — a clean seat is required before a child runs` };
   106	  }
   107	  const head = (await git(io, path, ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'])).stdout.trim();
   108	  if (head !== target.branch) {
   109	    return { ok: false, check: 'wrong-branch', refusal: `worktree ${path} HEAD is on '${head}', expected branch '${target.branch}'` };
   110	  }
   111	  return { ok: true };
   112	}
   113	
   114	/**
   115	 * Postflight after a child: HEAD still on the expected branch (checked in the
   116	 * seat cwd) and main unmoved from the pre-child capture.
   117	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string, mainSha: string}} target @param {any} io
   118	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, check: string, refusal: string}>}
   119	 */
   120	export async function postflightWorktree(root, target, io) {
   121	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
   122	  const head = (await git(io, path, ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'])).stdout.trim();
   123	  if (head !== target.branch) {
   124	    return { ok: false, check: 'head-drift', refusal: `the child left HEAD on '${head}' instead of the expected branch '${target.branch}'` };
   125	  }
   126	  const main = (await git(io, root, ['rev-parse', 'main'])).stdout.trim();
   127	  if (main !== target.mainSha) {
   128	    return { ok: false, check: 'main-moved', refusal: `main moved under the child (was ${target.mainSha}, now ${main}) — only the merger writes main` };
   129	  }
   130	  return { ok: true };
   131	}
   132	
   133	/**
   134	 * Full-range attribution scan over main..branch — the merge gate. Every
   135	 * commit must be authored AND committed by the sole author, with no forbidden
   136	 * AI-attribution trailer in the body. The range is never windowed.
   137	 * @param {string} root @param {string} branch @param {any} io
   138	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, offending: string, reason: string}>}
   139	 */
   140	export async function attributionScan(root, branch, io) {
   141	  const out = (await git(io, root, ['log', `main..${branch}`, `--format=${LOG_FORMAT}`])).stdout;
   142	  for (const record of String(out).split('\x1e')) {
   143	    if (record.trim().length === 0) continue;
   144	    const [sha, an, ae, cn, ce, body = ''] = record.replace(/^\n/, '').split('\x00');
   145	    if (an !== SOLE_AUTHOR.name || ae !== SOLE_AUTHOR.email) {
   146	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} has a foreign author identity (${an} <${ae}>) — sole-author is required` };
   147	    }
   148	    if (cn !== SOLE_AUTHOR.name || ce !== SOLE_AUTHOR.email) {
   149	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} has a foreign committer identity (${cn} <${ce}>) — sole-author is required` };
   150	    }
   151	    if (TRAILER_PATTERNS.some((re) => re.test(body))) {
   152	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} carries a forbidden AI-attribution trailer (co-authored/generated-with/emoji) in its message` };
   153	    }
   154	  }
   155	  return { ok: true };
   156	}
   157	
   158	const mergeLockPath = (/** @type {string} */ root) => `${root}/.handoff/loop/merge.lock`;
   159	
   160	/**
   161	 * Merge an approved subtask branch into main — one guarded transaction:
   162	 * acquire the merge lock (a held lock refuses), gate on the attribution scan,
   163	 * merge at the repo root, abort-and-park on conflict, then ff-only sync both
   164	 * seats. The lock is released on EVERY exit path after acquisition.
   165	 * @param {string} root @param {{branch: string}} input @param {any} io
   166	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true, branch: string} | {ok: false, reason: string}>}
   167	 */
   168	export async function mergeSubtask(root, input, io) {
   169	  const lock = mergeLockPath(root);
   170	  if (io.fs.existsSync(lock)) {
   171	    return { ok: false, reason: `the merge lock at ${lock} is held by another merger — refusing a concurrent merge` };
   172	  }
   173	  ensureDir(io.fs, `${root}/.handoff/loop`);
   174	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, lock, { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, at: io.now() });
   175	  try {
   176	    const scan = await attributionScan(root, input.branch, io);
   177	    if (scan.ok !== true) return { ok: false, reason: scan.reason };
   178	
   179	    try {
   180	      await git(io, root, ['merge', input.branch]);
   181	    } catch (err) {
   182	      // Conflict (or any merge failure): abort, never auto-resolve — park.
   183	      try {
   184	        await git(io, root, ['merge', '--abort']);
   185	      } catch {
   186	        // Nothing to abort — the failure predates a merge state.
   187	      }
   188	      const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.stdout || /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message || String(err);
   189	      return { ok: false, reason: `merge conflict — aborted and parked for the operator: ${String(msg).split('\n')[0]}` };
   190	    }
   191	
   192	    const seats = worktreePaths(root).seats;
   193	    for (const seat of SEATS) {
   194	      try {
   195	        await git(io, seats[seat], ['merge', '--ff-only', 'main']);
   196	      } catch (err) {
   197	        const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.stderr || /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message || String(err);
   198	        return { ok: false, reason: `worktree wt-${seat} could not fast-forward to main — corruption signal, parked: ${String(msg).split('\n')[0]}` };
   199	      }
   200	    }
   201	    return { ok: true, branch: input.branch };
   202	  } finally {
   203	    try {
   204	      if (io.fs.existsSync(lock)) io.fs.unlinkSync(lock);
   205	    } catch {
   206	      // Best effort — a leaked lock is visible and operator-recoverable.
   207	    }
   208	  }
   209	}
   210	
   211	/**
   212	 * Self-heal a raw-deleted seat: git still lists it but the directory is gone
   213	 * — prune the stale metadata and re-add on the same branch.
   214	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string}} target @param {any} io
   215	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, healed: boolean}>}
   216	 */
   217	export async function selfHealWorktree(root, target, io) {
   218	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
   219	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
   220	  const stale = listed.some((e) => e.path === path) && !io.fs.existsSync(path);
   221	  if (!stale) return { ok: true, healed: false };
   222	  await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'prune']);
   223	  await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'add', path, target.branch]);
   224	  return { ok: true, healed: true };
   225	}
   226	
   227	/**
   228	 * Tear down BOTH seats: remove the worktrees and delete their namespaced
   229	 * branches. Anything outside .worktrees/wt-* or baton/wt-* is never touched,
   230	 * so a teardown leaves the repo a plain git repo (acceptance constraint 5).
   231	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   232	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, removed: string[]}>}
   233	 */
   234	export async function teardownWorktrees(root, io) {
   235	  const p = worktreePaths(root);
   236	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
   237	  /** @type {string[]} */
   238	  const removed = [];
   239	  for (const seat of SEATS) {
   240	    const entry = listed.find((e) => e.path === p.seats[seat]);
   241	    if (!entry) continue;
   242	    await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'remove', '--force', p.seats[seat]]);
   243	    // Delete the seat's CURRENT branch and its setup base branch — a seat
   244	    // that moved on to subtask branches orphans its base otherwise
   245	    // (acceptance constraint 5: teardown leaves a plain git repo). Only
   246	    // namespaced branches are ever deleted.
   247	    /** @type {Set<string>} */
   248	    const toDelete = new Set();
   249	    if (entry.branch && WORKTREE_BRANCH_RE.test(entry.branch)) toDelete.add(entry.branch);
   250	    toDelete.add(seatBranch(seat));
   251	    for (const branch of toDelete) {
   252	      if (!WORKTREE_BRANCH_RE.test(branch)) continue;
   253	      try {
   254	        await git(io, root, ['branch', '-D', branch]);
   255	      } catch {
   256	        // Already gone (current === base, or deleted earlier) — fine.
   257	      }
   258	    }
   259	    removed.push(seat);
   260	  }
   261	  return { ok: true, removed };
   262	}
   263	
   264	/**
   265	 * Branch jail: only baton/wt-* branches may ever be deleted through this
   266	 * layer. An out-of-namespace ref refuses without touching git.
   267	 * @param {string} root @param {string} branch @param {any} io
   268	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, error: string}>}
   269	 */
   270	export async function deleteBranch(root, branch, io) {
   271	  if (!WORKTREE_BRANCH_RE.test(branch)) {
   272	    return { ok: false, error: `refused: '${branch}' is outside the baton/wt- namespace — the loop never deletes non-loop branches` };
   273	  }
   274	  await git(io, root, ['branch', '-D', branch]);
   275	  return { ok: true };
   276	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Loop run state — snapshot + journal + replay for the Layer-2 supervisor,
     3	 * mirroring the bundle store's crash-tolerance idioms (atomic tmp+rename
     4	 * snapshots, torn-tail-tolerant NDJSON, seq-dedup replay). Everything lives
     5	 * under <root>/.handoff/loop/; the supervisor is the sole writer. Plan:
     6	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"baton loop",
     7	 * §"Smoke gate", §"Supervisor lifetime and recovery", §"Caps".
     8	 */
     9	import { appendEntry, readAllTolerant } from '../util/jsonl.mjs';
    10	import { atomicWriteJson, safeReadJson, ensureDir } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
    11	import { dedupeKey } from '../util/ids.mjs';
    12	
    13	export const LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA = 'baton/loop-state@1';
    14	
    15	/** The frozen status vocabulary. */
    16	export const LOOP_STATUS = Object.freeze({
    17	  RUNNING: 'running',
    18	  AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL: 'awaiting-smoke-approval',
    19	  PARKED: 'parked',
    20	  ESCALATED: 'escalated',
    21	  DONE: 'done',
    22	});
    23	
    24	/** The frozen journal event vocabulary. */
    25	export const LOOP_EVENT = Object.freeze({
    26	  PHASE_ADVANCE: 'phase-advance',
    27	  GATE_ITERATION: 'gate-iteration',
    28	  PARK: 'park',
    29	  RESUME: 'resume',
    30	  SMOKE_AWAIT: 'smoke-await',
    31	  SMOKE_APPROVE: 'smoke-approve',
    32	});
    33	
    34	// Every gate loops at most 5 times — a repo-wide hard invariant (AGENTS.md
    35	// §Review gates); the reducer refuses a 6th and escalates instead.
    36	const GATE_ITERATION_CAP = 5;
    37	
    38	/** @param {string} root */
    39	export function loopPaths(root) {
    40	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff/loop`;
    41	  return { dir, state: `${dir}/state.json`, journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson` };
    42	}
    43	
    44	/**
    45	 * Fresh run state — pure and deterministic from the injected io (identity
    46	 * facts + clock only; no Date.now/Math.random, which would break replay).
    47	 * @param {any} spec a VALIDATED loop spec @param {any} io
    48	 */
    49	export function initLoopState(spec, io) {
    50	  const runId = `loop-${dedupeKey({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, at: io.now() }).slice(0, 12)}`;
    51	  /** @type {Record<string, number>} */
    52	  const iterations = {};
    53	  for (const p of spec.phases ?? []) {
    54	    if (/gate/.test(String(p.id))) iterations[p.id] = 0;
    55	  }
    56	  return {
    57	    schema: LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA,
    58	    runId,
    59	    goal: spec.goal,
    60	    phaseCount: Array.isArray(spec.phases) ? spec.phases.length : 0,
    61	    phaseIndex: 0,
    62	    iterations,
    63	    status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING,
    64	    parkReason: null,
    65	    escalation: null,
    66	    smokeApproval: null,
    67	    createdAt: io.now(),
    68	    journalSeq: 0,
    69	  };
    70	}
    71	
    72	/**
    73	 * The pure transition reducer. Never mutates its input; unknown events and
    74	 * refused transitions return the input state unchanged (a new reference is
    75	 * only produced for a real transition).
    76	 * @param {any} state @param {any} ev
    77	 */
    78	export function applyLoopEvent(state, ev) {
    79	  switch (ev?.type) {
    80	    case LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE: {
    81	      const next = state.phaseIndex + 1;
    82	      if (next >= state.phaseCount) return { ...state, phaseIndex: state.phaseCount, status: LOOP_STATUS.DONE };
    83	      return { ...state, phaseIndex: next };
    84	    }
    85	    case LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION: {
    86	      const gate = String(ev.gate);
    87	      const current = state.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0;
    88	      // The 6th attempt is REFUSED: the counter stays at the cap and the run
    89	      // escalates with a record naming the gate — never a 6th run.
    90	      if (current >= GATE_ITERATION_CAP) {
    91	        return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, escalation: { gate, iteration: current } };
    92	      }
    93	      return { ...state, iterations: { ...state.iterations, [gate]: current + 1 } };
    94	    }
    95	    case LOOP_EVENT.PARK:
    96	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, parkReason: ev.reason ?? null };
    97	    case LOOP_EVENT.RESUME:
    98	      // Only a PARK is resumable; an escalation is an operator decision.
    99	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) return state;
   100	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING, parkReason: null };
   101	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT:
   102	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL };
   103	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE: {
   104	      // Guarded transition: only meaningful while awaiting, and only with the
   105	      // caller's verifySmokeToken result stamped verified === true. A missing
   106	      // or false `verified` is unverified — it never resumes the run.
   107	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) return state;
   108	      if (ev.verified !== true) return state;
   109	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING, smokeApproval: { token: ev.token, at: ev.ts ?? null } };
   110	    }
   111	    default:
   112	      return state;
   113	  }
   114	}
   115	
   116	/**
   117	 * Atomic snapshot write (tmp + rename under .handoff/loop).
   118	 * @param {string} root @param {any} state @param {any} io
   119	 */
   120	export async function writeLoopState(root, state, io) {
   121	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   122	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   123	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, p.state, state);
   124	}
   125	
   126	/**
   127	 * Append one journal event; allocates the next seq past
   128	 * max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) unless the entry carries one.
   129	 * @param {string} root @param {any} ev @param {any} io
   130	 * @returns {Promise<number>} the persisted seq
   131	 */
   132	export async function appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io) {
   133	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   134	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   135	  let seq = ev.seq;
   136	  if (typeof seq !== 'number') {
   137	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.state);
   138	    const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   139	    let tailSeq = 0;
   140	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   141	      for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
   142	        if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
   143	      }
   144	    }
   145	    seq = Math.max(snapSeq, tailSeq) + 1;
   146	  }
   147	  const ts = typeof ev.ts === 'string' ? ev.ts : io.now();
   148	  const entry = {
   149	    ...ev,
   150	    seq,
   151	    ts,
   152	    dedupeKey: typeof ev.dedupeKey === 'string' && ev.dedupeKey.length > 0 ? ev.dedupeKey : dedupeKey({ seq, ts, type: ev.type, gate: ev.gate ?? null }),
   153	  };
   154	  appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, entry);
   155	  return seq;
   156	}
   157	
   158	/**
   159	 * Load = snapshot + torn-tail-tolerant replay of journal events PAST the
   160	 * snapshot's seq. Duplicate seqs/dedupeKeys apply exactly once, so a
   161	 * supervisor-death recovery never re-runs a completed iteration.
   162	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   163	 * @returns {Promise<{state: any, warnings: string[]}>}
   164	 */
   165	export async function loadLoopState(root, io) {
   166	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   167	  /** @type {string[]} */
   168	  const warnings = [];
   169	  const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.state);
   170	  if (!snap.ok) return { state: null, warnings: ['no loop state snapshot found'] };
   171	  let state = snap.value;
   172	  const baseSeq = typeof state.journalSeq === 'number' ? state.journalSeq : 0;
   173	
   174	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   175	    const { entries, warnings: jw } = readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal);
   176	    for (const w of jw ?? []) warnings.push(`journal: ${w.kind ?? 'warning'} at line ${w.line ?? '?'}`);
   177	    const applied = new Set();
   178	    let maxSeq = baseSeq;
   179	    for (const e of entries) {
   180	      const seq = typeof e.seq === 'number' ? e.seq : null;
   181	      if (seq !== null && seq <= baseSeq) continue; // already folded into the snapshot
   182	      const key = e.dedupeKey ?? (seq !== null ? `seq:${seq}` : null);
   183	      if (key !== null && applied.has(key)) continue; // replayed duplicate — apply once
   184	      if (seq !== null && applied.has(`seq:${seq}`)) continue;
   185	      state = applyLoopEvent(state, e);
   186	      if (key !== null) applied.add(key);
   187	      if (seq !== null) {
   188	        applied.add(`seq:${seq}`);
   189	        if (seq > maxSeq) maxSeq = seq;
   190	      }
   191	    }
   192	    state = { ...state, journalSeq: maxSeq };
   193	  }
   194	  return { state, warnings };
   195	}
   196	
   197	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   198	// Smoke-approval token — a STRUCTURED digest over the six bound inputs, so a
   199	// stale approval is refused NAMING what moved (mirrors the receive receipt
   200	// token's driftedInputs discipline).
   201	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   202	
   203	const SMOKE_TOKEN_PREFIX = 'smk1';
   204	const SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS = /** @type {const} */ ([
   205	  'stateDigest',
   206	  'smokeCmd',
   207	  'smokeOutputDigest',
   208	  'gitHead',
   209	  'gitContentDigest',
   210	  'childAssignment',
   211	]);
   212	
   213	/**
   214	 * Deterministic structured token. Per-field digests keep field boundaries
   215	 * (no concatenation collisions) and let verification name drifted inputs.
   216	 * Missing bound fields throw — a token over partial inputs is meaningless.
   217	 * @param {Record<string, string>} inputs
   218	 * @returns {string}
   219	 */
   220	export function smokeApprovalToken(inputs) {
   221	  for (const f of SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS) {
   222	    if (typeof inputs?.[f] !== 'string') throw new Error(`smoke approval token: required input '${f}' is missing`);
   223	  }
   224	  return `${SMOKE_TOKEN_PREFIX}.` + SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.map((f) => dedupeKey(inputs[f]).slice(0, 10)).join('.');
   225	}
   226	
   227	/**
   228	 * Recompute against current inputs; positional comparison names every
   229	 * drifted input. Never throws — partial current inputs simply cannot verify.
   230	 * @param {string} presented @param {Record<string, string>} inputs
   231	 * @returns {{ok: true} | {ok: false, driftedInputs: string[]}}
   232	 */
   233	export function verifySmokeToken(presented, inputs) {
   234	  /** @type {string} */
   235	  let current;
   236	  try {
   237	    current = smokeApprovalToken(inputs);
   238	  } catch {
   239	    return { ok: false, driftedInputs: SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.filter((f) => typeof inputs?.[f] !== 'string') };
   240	  }
   241	  if (presented === current) return { ok: true };
   242	  const a = String(presented ?? '').split('.');
   243	  const b = current.split('.');
   244	  /** @type {string[]} */
   245	  const drifted = [];
   246	  SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.forEach((f, i) => {
   247	    if (a[i + 1] !== b[i + 1]) drifted.push(f);
   248	  });
   249	  return { ok: false, driftedInputs: drifted.length > 0 ? drifted : [...SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS] };
   250	}

## Deferred from v1 of this milestone (Gate-2 fold, recorded 2026-07-19)

Explicitly deferred — recorded so the shipped surface is never read as
implementing them (Gate-2 findings G8–G13): `loop run --detach` (the
supervisor is foreground-only in v1; recovery via the run lock + journal is
implemented and tested); probe-cache integration in the loop/pipeline
resolvers (probes: null — resolution is degraded-flagged offline behavior);
a smoke gate in pipeline mode (the smoke gate is a loop-mode surface);
findings persistence across supervisor restarts (findings are in-memory per
invocation; the journal carries gate iterations, not findings text);
reviews/ artifact generation from loop children; per-phase (vs global)
maxChildrenPerPhase accounting; a live-transcript codex marker fixture;
streaming log caps + head/tail classification buffers; child-log secret
redaction + inclusion in the purge walk; deriving the trunk branch instead
of assuming 'main'. These are v1.1 hardening items unless a gate re-raises
one as blocking.

## Out of scope for this milestone

Multi-repo loops; parallel subtask fan-out (>1 writer at once); cloud/cron
driving (the supervisor is local-first; scheduled runs are a later layer);
LLM-judged smoke verdicts (the smoke gate is a human gate by decision 3).

     1	// Fake `io` object matching the injected-io contract from docs/design/core.md:
     2	// { cwd, env, stdin, stdout, stderr, fs, execFile, now }. Backed by memfs.
     3	// stdout/stderr collect writes; io.stdoutText()/io.stderrText() flatten them.
     4	// now() returns a settable ISO string (io.setNow). execFile resolves from an
     5	// execResults map keyed by the joined command string.
     6	//
     7	// Wave-B2 additions (bundle lock layer) — all optional, defaulted, and purely
     8	// additive so no prior consumer is affected:
     9	//   host / pid / startTime  — the process-identity facts the lock model writes
    10	//     into owner.json and compares on contention (see plan §Concurrency, Lock
    11	//     model iterations 3+4).
    12	//   processAlive(pid, startTime) — the "provably dead" oracle. Default: only
    13	//     THIS io's own {pid, startTime} is alive; every other descriptor reads as
    14	//     dead. Override per test to model a live foreign owner or a pid/start-time
    15	//     mismatch (reused pid).
    16	//   newFencingToken() — the injected per-acquisition token source. Deterministic
    17	//     monotonic counter ("tok-1", "tok-2", …), matching the injected-io
    18	//     discipline already used for now()/execFile so lock acquisition is
    19	//     reproducible in tests rather than drawing from Math.random.
    20	
    21	import { makeMemfs } from './memfs.mjs';
    22	
    23	export function makeIo({
    24	  files = {},
    25	  env = {},
    26	  stdin = '',
    27	  execResults = {},
    28	  now = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z',
    29	  host = 'host-A',
    30	  pid = 4242,
    31	  startTime = 111000,
    32	  processAlive = null,
    33	  fencingSeed = 1,
    34	  execPath = process.execPath,
    35	} = {}) {
    36	  const { fs, files: filesSnapshot } = makeMemfs(files);
    37	  const outChunks = [];
    38	  const errChunks = [];
    39	  let currentNow = now;
    40	  let tokenCounter = fencingSeed - 1;
    41	  const defaultAlive = (p, s) => p === pid && s === startTime;
    42	
    43	  const stdout = { write: (s) => { outChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
    44	  const stderr = { write: (s) => { errChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
    45	
    46	  const execFile = (cmd, args = [], /* opts */ _opts = {}) => {
    47	    const key = [cmd, ...(Array.isArray(args) ? args : [])].join(' ');
    48	    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    49	      if (!(key in execResults)) {
    50	        const e = new Error(`spawn ${cmd} ENOENT`);
    51	        e.code = 'ENOENT';
    52	        return reject(e);
    53	      }
    54	      const r = execResults[key];
    55	      const code = r.code ?? 0;
    56	      const out = r.stdout ?? '';
    57	      const err = r.stderr ?? '';
    58	      if (code !== 0) {
    59	        // util.promisify(execFile) rejects on nonzero exit; error carries code + streams.
    60	        const e = new Error(`Command failed: ${key}`);
    61	        e.code = code;
    62	        e.stdout = out;
    63	        e.stderr = err;
    64	        return reject(e);
    65	      }
    66	      resolve({ stdout: out, stderr: err });
    67	    });
    68	  };
    69	
    70	  return {
    71	    cwd: '/repo',
    72	    env,
    73	    stdin,
    74	    stdout,
    75	    stderr,
    76	    fs,
    77	    execFile,
    78	    now: () => currentNow,
    79	    setNow: (iso) => { currentNow = iso; },
    80	    execPath,
    81	    host,
    82	    pid,
    83	    startTime,
    84	    processAlive: processAlive || defaultAlive,
    85	    // Tiny retry budget: unit tests exercising live-lock refusal stay fast
    86	    // while the retry code path itself still runs (production default: 120x25ms).
    87	    lockRetry: { attempts: 2, delayMs: 2 },
    88	    newFencingToken: () => `tok-${++tokenCounter}`,
    89	    stdoutText: () => outChunks.join(''),
    90	    stderrText: () => errChunks.join(''),
    91	    files: filesSnapshot,
    92	  };
    93	}
