   833	// H2 (A1/B2) — bounded failover: avoidEntries/avoid carry; exhaustion PARKS.
   834	describe('pipeline — H2: bounded failover (avoid carries; exhaustion parks)', () => {
   835	  // A recording runner that emits a platform-appropriate death banner and THROWS
   836	  // once a bound is exceeded, so an unbounded ping-pong terminates the test RED.
   837	  function boundedDeathRunner(io, { bound, banner }) {
   838	    const calls = [];
   839	    const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
   840	      calls.push({ spec, command: spec?.command, args: (spec?.args ?? []).map(String) });
   841	      if (calls.length > bound) throw new Error(`unbounded failover: exceeded ${bound} child spawns (ping-pong)`);
   842	      const text = typeof banner === 'function' ? banner(spec) : banner;
   843	      io.fs.mkdirSync(opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/')), { recursive: true });
   844	      io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, `working...\n${text}\n`);
   845	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 1, verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: '', logPath: opts.logPath });
   846	    };
   847	    fn.calls = calls;
   848	    return fn;
   849	  }
   850	  const modelsOf = (runner) => runner.calls.filter((c) => valAfter(c.args, '-s') === 'workspace-write').map((c) => valAfter(c.args, '--model'));
   851	
   852	  it('RED (H2a): always-model-unavailable on a two-entry codex chain never re-picks a rejected model; all rejected -> PARK (bounded)', async () => {

   855	    const runner = boundedDeathRunner(io, { bound: 3, banner: MODEL_UNAVAIL }); // correct impl parks at 2 writers
   856	    io.superviseChild = runner;

   585	// ===========================================================================
   586	// G6 (B6) — `baton loop resume`: PARKED -> running; ESCALATED refused.
   587	describe('loop run — resume (G6)', () => {
   588	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
   589	  const seedState = (over) => ({
   590	    schema: 'baton/loop-state@1',
   591	    runId: 'loop-r',
   592	    goal: 'Ship the loop',
   593	    phaseCount: 2,
   594	    phaseIndex: 0,
   595	    iterations: {},
   596	    status: 'running',
   597	    parkReason: null,
   598	    escalation: null,
   599	    smokeApproval: null,
   600	    createdAt: T0,
   601	    journalSeq: 0,
   602	    ...over,
   603	  });
   604	
   605	  it('RED (G6): `baton loop resume` transitions a PARKED run back to running and continues (spawns the pending phase)', async () => {
   606	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   607	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   608	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   609	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   610	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/state.json`, JSON.stringify(seedState({ status: 'parked', parkReason: 'operator paused' }), null, 2) + '\n');
   611	
   612	    const code = await cmdLoop(['resume'], io);
   613	    assert.equal(code, 0, `resume continues a parked run; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   614	    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'resume spawned the pending phase');
   615	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the resumed run completes');
   616	  });
   617	
   618	  it('RED (G6): resume on an ESCALATED run is REFUSED (escalation stays operator-only, no spawn)', async () => {
   619	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   620	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   621	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   622	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   623	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/state.json`, JSON.stringify(seedState({ status: 'escalated', escalation: { gate: 'gate-1', iteration: 5 } }), null, 2) + '\n');
   624	
   625	    const code = await cmdLoop(['resume'], io);
   626	    assert.notEqual(code, 0, 'an escalated run cannot be resumed');
   627	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /escalat|operator/i, 'the refusal explains escalation is operator-only');
   628	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'resume never spawns on an escalated run');
   629	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, 'the run stays escalated');
   630	  });
   631	});

   633	// ===========================================================================
   634	// H3 (A2/B5) — children.ndjson RETIRES completed children; reclaim kills only
   635	// still-in-flight groups (never an OS-recycled pid of a retired child).
   636	describe('loop run — child registry retirement (H3)', () => {
   637	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
   638	  const readChildren = (io) => {
   639	    const raw = io.files()[`${DIR}/children.ndjson`];
   640	    if (typeof raw !== 'string') return [];
   641	    return raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
   642	  };
   643	
   644	  it('RED (H3a): after a clean run, EVERY started child has a matching retire record ({childId, endedAt})', async () => {
   645	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   646	    // A runner that reports its group (so the supervisor writes the START record
   647	    // via its onStart handler); the RETIRE record is the supervisor's job on resolve.
   648	    let n = 0;
   649	    const notRetiredAtStart = [];
   650	    io.superviseChild = (/** @type {any} */ _spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
   651	      n += 1;
   652	      const pgid = 9000 + n;
   653	      if (typeof opts.onStart === 'function') opts.onStart({ pid: 8000 + n, pgid });
   654	      // Ordering: the child is IN FLIGHT here — it must have a start record and
   655	      // NO retire yet (retirement happens only on resolution).
   656	      const start = readChildren(io).find((r) => r.pgid === pgid);
   657	      const retiredEarly = readChildren(io).some((r) => start && r.childId === start.childId && typeof r.endedAt === 'string');
   658	      notRetiredAtStart.push(!!start && !retiredEarly);
   659	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '', logPath: opts.logPath, pgid });
   660	    };
   661	    io.__runner = { calls: [] };
   662	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   663	    assert.ok(notRetiredAtStart.length >= 1 && notRetiredAtStart.every(Boolean), 'a just-started child has a start record and NO endedAt while in flight');
   664	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   665	    const recs = readChildren(io);
   666	    const started = recs.filter((r) => typeof r.pgid === 'number').map((r) => r.childId);
   667	    const retired = recs.filter((r) => typeof r.endedAt === 'string').map((r) => r.childId);
   668	    assert.ok(started.length >= 1, 'at least one child started');
   669	    for (const id of started) assert.ok(retired.includes(id), `child ${id} was retired (a {childId, endedAt} record exists)`);
   670	  });
   671	
   672	  it('RED (H3b): a dead-lock reclaim KILLS NOTHING for a RETIRED child, even when processAlive says its (recycled) pgid is alive', async () => {
   673	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   674	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   675	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   676	    const killCalls = [];
   677	    io.processKill = (/** @type {number} */ pid, /** @type {any} */ sig) => { killCalls.push({ pid, sig }); return true; };
   678	    // The prior run's child is RETIRED, but its pgid was recycled by an unrelated live process.
   679	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ p) => p === 9001;
   680	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   681	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'crashed' }));
   682	    io.fs.writeFileSync(
   683	      `${DIR}/children.ndjson`,
   684	      JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 8001, pgid: 9001, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n' + JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', endedAt: T0 }) + '\n',
   685	    );
   686	
   687	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   688	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the reclaiming run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   689	    assert.ok(!killCalls.some((k) => Math.abs(k.pid) === 9001), `a retired child is never killed (pid-reuse safety); kills: ${JSON.stringify(killCalls)}`);
   690	  });
   691	});
   692	
   693	// ===========================================================================
   694	// H5 (B4) vice-versa — a pipeline-flavored state is refused by `loop run`.
   695	describe('loop run — state flavor binding (H5)', () => {
   696	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
   697	  it('RED (H5): a state.json created by `pipeline run` (flavor pipeline) is REFUSED by loop run (exit 2), zero spawns', async () => {
   698	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   699	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);

   706	        iterations: {}, status: 'running', parkReason: null, escalation: null, smokeApproval: null, createdAt: T0, journalSeq: 0,
   707	        flavor: 'pipeline',
   708	      }, null, 2) + '\n',
   709	    );
   710	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   711	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'a pipeline-flavored state cannot be resumed as a loop');
   712	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /flavor|pipeline|mismatch/i, 'the refusal names the flavor mismatch');
   713	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned on a flavor mismatch');
   714	  });
   715	
   716	  it('RED (H5): a resume whose specDigest was computed over DIFFERENT phases REFUSES (exit 2), zero spawns', async () => {
   717	    // The current loop.json has plan -> gate-1; the seeded state was digested over
   718	    // a different phase list, so resuming its phaseIndex would misalign.
   719	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   720	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);

   722	    const oldPhases = [{ id: 'plan', role: 'planner' }, { id: 'REMOVED', role: 'plan-reviewer' }, { id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }];
   723	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   724	    io.fs.writeFileSync(
   725	      `${DIR}/state.json`,
   726	      JSON.stringify({
   727	        schema: 'baton/loop-state@1', runId: 'loop-seeded', goal: 'Ship the loop', phaseCount: 2, phaseIndex: 1,
   728	        iterations: {}, status: 'running', parkReason: null, escalation: null, smokeApproval: null, createdAt: T0, journalSeq: 0,
   729	        flavor: 'loop', specDigest: dedupeKey(oldPhases),
   730	      }, null, 2) + '\n',
   731	    );
   732	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   733	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'a resume against a changed phase list refuses rather than misaligning');
   734	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /phase|spec|changed|digest/i, 'the refusal names the spec change');
   735	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned on a spec-digest mismatch');
   736	  });

codex
VERDICT: BLOCKED
FINDINGS:
1. [blocking] — `core/src/commands/pipeline.mjs:237` auto-advances any subtask branch whose tip is an ancestor of `main`; a stale empty branch created at `main` also satisfies that, bypassing the stale-branch park and empty-branch refusal, so `pipeline run` can skip a subtask without writer/reviewer/merge work — Fix: only auto-advance with supervisor-owned proof of a completed merge, such as a persisted per-subtask merge receipt/intent containing pre-merge `main` and branch tip, and otherwise fall through to stale-branch parking.
2. [major] — H5 state binding only refuses present-but-mismatched `flavor`/`specDigest`; an existing unstamped `state.json` is still accepted by both loop and pipeline, preserving the unbound shared-state failure mode for legacy/malformed state — Fix: treat missing `flavor` or `specDigest` on existing state as a binding error or explicit archive/migration path, with zero-spawn pins.
3. [minor] — `loop run` can return on H5 mismatch after acquiring `supervisor.lock` but before entering the `finally`, leaking the lock until the next reclaim — Fix: move state binding checks inside the lock `try/finally` or explicitly release before those early returns.
tokens used
142,239
VERDICT: BLOCKED
FINDINGS:
1. [blocking] — `core/src/commands/pipeline.mjs:237` auto-advances any subtask branch whose tip is an ancestor of `main`; a stale empty branch created at `main` also satisfies that, bypassing the stale-branch park and empty-branch refusal, so `pipeline run` can skip a subtask without writer/reviewer/merge work — Fix: only auto-advance with supervisor-owned proof of a completed merge, such as a persisted per-subtask merge receipt/intent containing pre-merge `main` and branch tip, and otherwise fall through to stale-branch parking.
2. [major] — H5 state binding only refuses present-but-mismatched `flavor`/`specDigest`; an existing unstamped `state.json` is still accepted by both loop and pipeline, preserving the unbound shared-state failure mode for legacy/malformed state — Fix: treat missing `flavor` or `specDigest` on existing state as a binding error or explicit archive/migration path, with zero-spawn pins.
3. [minor] — `loop run` can return on H5 mismatch after acquiring `supervisor.lock` but before entering the `finally`, leaking the lock until the next reclaim — Fix: move state binding checks inside the lock `try/finally` or explicitly release before those early returns.
